def generar_docx(data): fecha = data.get("fecha","") nro_res = data.get("nroRes","") nro_exp = data.get("nroExp","") paciente = data.get("paciente","") insumo = data.get("insumo","") precio_raw = data.get("precio","").replace(".","").replace(",","").strip() imp_letras = data.get("importeLetras","") n = int(precio_raw) fmt = "{:,}".format(n).replace(",",".") precio_unit = "$ " + fmt precio_total = "$ " + fmt + ",00" importe = "$ " + fmt + ", 00" buf = io.BytesIO() with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout: for name, b64 in TEMPLATE.items(): raw = base64.b64decode(b64) if name == "[Content_Types].xml": xml = raw.decode("utf-8") xml = xml.replace( "application/vnd.ms-word.template.macroEnabledTemplate.main+xml", "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document.main+xml" ) raw = xml.encode("utf-8") elif name == "word/document.xml": xml = raw.decode("utf-8") xml = xml.replace("__FECHA__", fecha) xml = xml.replace("__NRO_RES__", nro_res) xml = xml.replace("__NRO_EXP__", nro_exp) xml = xml.replace("__PACIENTE__", paciente) xml = xml.replace("__INSUMO_CORTO__", insumo) xml = xml.replace("__PRECIO_UNIT__", precio_unit) xml = xml.replace("__PRECIO_TOTAL__", precio_total) xml = xml.replace("__IMPORTE__", importe) xml = xml.replace("__IMPORTE_LETRAS__", imp_letras) raw = xml.encode("utf-8") zout.writestr(name, raw) return buf.getvalue() class handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler): def do_POST(self): length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0)) body = self.rfile.read(length) data = json.loads(body) try: docx = generar_docx(data) apellido = data.get("paciente","").split(",")[0].strip().replace(" ","_") nombre = f"Resolucion_{data.get('nroRes','0')}_{apellido}.docx" self.send_response(200) self.send_header("Content-Type","application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document") self.send_header("Content-Disposition", f'attachment; filename="{nombre}"') self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(docx))) self.end_headers() self.wfile.write(docx) except Exception as e: self.send_response(500) self.send_header("Content-Type","application/json") self.end_headers() self.wfile.write(json.dumps({"error": str(e)}).encode())
